<article class="node node--type-landing-page node--promoted node--non-sticky-sidebar node--display-mode-full updates-text-color-body c-article">
  
  
      <div>
        
    
          

      <h2 class="node-subtitle field">
      




<div class="c-field c-field--name-field-subtitle c-field--type-string-long c-field--label-hidden">
  <div class="c-field__content"><div class="c-field__item">NOAA experts provide data, tools and information to help people understand, prepare for and adapt to our changing climate.</div></div></div>

    </h2>
      
    
    
          


<div class="c-field c-field--name-field-updates c-field--type-entity-reference-revisions c-field--label-hidden">
  <div class="c-field__content"><div class="c-field__items"><div class="c-field__item">  


  


<section class="l-section paragraph paragraph--type-update-notes paragraph--display-mode-default MIN XXS XS SM MD LG XL" data-observe-resizes="" data-observing="true">
  
    
  <div class="l-section__content">
          <div id="paragraph-id-40283">
        <span class="update-date"><span class="update-label">UPDATED: </span>June 24, 2025.</span>
                  <span class="update-notes">In compliance with Executive Order 14303 (“Restoring Gold Standard Science”), the White House Office of Science and Technology Policy’s June 23, 2025 Memorandum (“Agency Guidance for Implementing Gold Standard Science in the Conduct &amp; Management of Scientific Activities”), 15 USC § 2904 (“National Climate Program”), 15 USC § 2934 (“National Global Change Research Plan”), and 33 USC § 893a (“NOAA Ocean and Atmospheric Science Education Programs”), you have been redirected to NOAA.gov. Future research products previously housed under Climate.gov will be available at NOAA.gov/climate and its affiliate websites.</span>
                
      </div>
      </div>

  </section>

</div></div></div></div>

<div class="c-field c-field--name-field-title c-field--type-string c-field--label-hidden">
  <div class="c-field__content"><div class="c-field__item">$2.780 trillion</div></div></div>
</div>
      <div class="fact-description">




<div class="c-field c-field--name-field-body-formatted-long c-field--type-text-long c-field--label-hidden">
  <div class="c-field__content"><div class="c-field__item"><p>From 1980 to August 2024, the U.S. has experienced 396 weather and climate disasters where overall damages reached at least $1 billion. The total cost of these events exceeds $2.780 trillion.</p>

  <div class="c-field__content"><div class="c-field__item">55</div></div></div>

<div class="c-field c-field--name-field-body-formatted-long c-field--type-text-long c-field--label-hidden">
  <div class="c-field__content"><div class="c-field__item"><p>The number of different atmospheric gases that NOAA measures in both atmospheric distribution and trends</p>

      <div class="big-fact">




<div class="c-field c-field--name-field-title c-field--type-string c-field--label-hidden">
  <div class="c-field__content"><div class="c-field__item">A 36-day cyclone</div></div></div>
</div>
      <div class="fact-description">




<div class="c-field c-field--name-field-body-formatted-long c-field--type-text-long c-field--label-hidden">
  <div class="c-field__content"><div class="c-field__item"><p>The longest-lived tropical cyclone (hurricane) on record was Cyclone Freddy, which lasted 36 days and crossed the Indian Ocean from Australia to Africa from February 4 – March 14, 2023.&nbsp;&nbsp;</p>
</div></div></div>

    </div>
    </div>

  </section></div></div><div class="slick-slide" data-slick-index="3" aria-hidden="true" style="width: 930px; position: relative; left: -2790px; top: 0; z-index: 998; opacity: 0;"><div><section class="l-section paragraph paragraph--type-quick-fact-item paragraph--display-mode-default MIN XXS XS SM MD LG" data-observe-resizes="" style="width: 100%; display: inline-block;" data-observing="true">

<div class="c-field c-field--name-field-title c-field--type-string c-field--label-hidden">
  <div class="c-field__content"><div class="c-field__item">19</div></div></div>

<div class="c-field c-field--name-field-body-formatted-long c-field--type-text-long c-field--label-hidden">
  <div class="c-field__content"><div class="c-field__item"><p>The number of La Nina events across the tropical Pacific Ocean since 1950. La Ninas cool the waters of the central and eastern Tropical Pacific Ocean affecting the air above it, starting a domino-effect in the atmosphere impacting the climate worldwide.</p>
</div></div></div>

  <div class="c-field__content"><div class="c-field__item"><p>The number of ocean temperature profiles generated by the Argo buoy fleet as of 2024. Argo buoys drift and dive throughout the ocean, recording temperature and salinity down to 2,000 meters (1.24 miles).</p>

  <div class="c-field__content"><div class="c-field__item">100 billion metric tons</div></div></div>

    </div>
    </div>

  </section></div></div><div class="slick-slide" data-slick-index="6" aria-hidden="true" style="width: 930px; position: relative; left: -5580px; top: 0; z-index: 998; opacity: 0;"><div><section class="l-section paragraph paragraph--type-quick-fact-item paragraph--display-mode-default MIN XXS XS SM MD LG" data-observe-resizes="" style="width: 100%; display: inline-block;" data-observing="true">
  
    
  <div class="l-section__content">
        <div id="paragraph-id-40311">
      <div class="big-fact">




<div class="c-field c-field--name-field-title c-field--type-string c-field--label-hidden">
  <div class="c-field__content"><div class="c-field__item">March 31</div></div></div>
</div>
      <div class="fact-description">




<div class="c-field c-field--name-field-body-formatted-long c-field--type-text-long c-field--label-hidden">
  <div class="c-field__content"><div class="c-field__item"><p>Only three states - Florida, Louisiana and Hawaii - have an average last freeze by March 31</p>
</div></div></div>
</div>
    </div>
    </div>

  </section></div></div></div></div></div>

      This survey is designed to measure your level of satisfaction with our website. It consists of seven questions and should take approximately two to five minutes to complete. <br><br>Please do not use this survey to provide comments on or responses to rules, notices, solicitations or other official agency actions. Any information you provide will be used for the sole purpose of improving NOAA's digital products and services. You may also send us an email at <a href="mailto:webmaster@noaa.gov">webmaster@noaa.gov</a>.

<br><br>


If you have comments concerning the accessibility of our website, please submit your feedback on our <a href="https://www.noaa.gov/accessibility">accessibility page</a>.
